- **Pendencies:** new `order_pendencies` + `add_/clear_/reopen_/remove_order_pendency` + `can_manage_order_pendencies` (`20270325000000_order_pendencies.sql`; RLS select-only, RPC writes, self-assertions). The convert dialog (`PiApproveOrderModal`) takes any number of free-text pendencies; they are saved right after the Order exists via `saveNewOrderPendencies` (approve_order_submission is NOT restated, so a failure never undoes the Order — the PI page says so). They show on the Order page inside Fabric & Finish (`OrderPendencies.tsx`, self-loading) as open reminders with Mark cleared / Remove, cleared ones under a disclosure with Reopen. Without the migration the Order page simply hides the block and the convert dialog's save reports the failure.
- **Commercial breakdown:** Excel-style bordered label|amount grid, subtle alternate-row tint, final row keeps its blue tint; the "fabric provided by BOE / client" sub-label is a small italic tag. CSS block appended at the end of globals.css.
- Proof: tsc clean; new `orderPendencies.test.tsx`; `orderProductCodes.test.ts`, `piVersionPdf.test.ts`, `piLineReview.render.test.tsx` updated to the new format. Migration replayed in a scratch Postgres 16 against STUBS of its dependencies (orders, users, auth.uid, the two authority functions): applies, re-applies idempotently, self-assertions pass; add/clear/reopen/remove, duplicate and blank handling, outsider refusal, approver permission, direct-insert refusal and cancelled-Order refusal all behaved. NOT proven against the real migration chain or production — run `supabase db push --linked --dry-run` and apply before merging.

## 2026-10-20 — Lead source is now required to submit a PI (PR open) · no migration
- Owner rule: Sales must choose the lead source before submitting, so management is no longer asked for it at approval (the workbook has no lead source to preselect from). Salesperson stays optional at Submit (the approval dialog preselects the workbook's exact match, PR #364).
- `ORDER_DETAILS_FIELDS.lead_source` need is now `submission`; `orderDetailsSubmissionGaps` names "Lead source" (blank or not one of the five); the Internal details field carries the red star; `buildPiCompletion` no longer lists it under "needed later".
- Enforcement is client-side (checklist + disabled Submit), like the other internal-detail gaps' display; the database authority for lead source remains `approve_order_submission`. PIs already submitted without one are unaffected and are asked once at approval. A PI returned for changes must have one before it is resubmitted.
- Tests: `salesOrderDetails.test.tsx`, `piCompletion.test.ts`, `piFormRedesign.render.test.tsx` updated.

## 2026-10-20 — Approval dialog preselects the salesperson the PI workbook names (PR open) · no migration
- Complaint: Create Confirmed Order asked for the BOE salesperson although the PI page showed "Salesperson named in PI workbook: <name>". Cause: that line is the workbook's typed author name; a PI Sales submitted without choosing a salesperson has `salesperson_id` NULL (and `lead_source` NULL), so the dialog opened empty. Dates were already prefilled and shown "From the PI".
- Change: when Sales saved nobody, the dialog preselects the BOE user whose name EXACTLY matches the workbook name (case/spacing ignored, whole name, exactly ONE active user: `matchWorkbookSalesperson` in `lib/orders/orderConfirmation.ts`). Shown as "From the PI workbook" with a Change link; no match, a partial name or two people with the same name still select nobody and show the name as a hint. Reverses the earlier "never match by name" rule on purpose (owner request); never the viewer or submitter; the database still validates the id.
- NOT changed: lead source has no source on a PI Sales left blank, so it is still asked at approval. Salesperson and lead source remain optional at Submit (`piCompletion.ts` "later" list) — making them required at Submit is a separate decision.
- Tests: `piApprovalModals.render.test.tsx` (2, 2b, 3) and `salesOrderDetails.test.tsx` updated.

## 2026-10-20 — Orders notifications from the database: app code re-landed (PR open) · no new migration (20270321000000/01 APPLIED to production 2026-10-20)
- The two migrations were applied from the main repo with `db push --linked --include-all` (dry run listed exactly those two), then verified: both in the migration list, the 6 new `notification_type` values and the 6 notification triggers present. This PR is the revert of #359 (the revert of #358's app code): hidden Orders types shown in the feed and badge, the PI page and the split-payment modal call `notifyFinance`, the finance notify route sends the new notices. The DB triggers have been live since the apply; a 2-minute dedup in `order_notify_users` keeps the browser notices from doubling them.
- Lesson kept: a PR that needs a migration stays Draft until the migration is verified applied. This one was opened after the apply.

## 2026-10-20 — Two HDFC accounts: HDFC PRI and HDFC SEC (PR open) · migration 20270324000000 → APPLIED to production 2026-10-20 (applied alone; the two 20270321 migrations were left unapplied)
- BOE holds two HDFC accounts but a payment could only say "HDFC". Payment modes are now `hdfc` (shown **HDFC PRI**, primary) and new `hdfc_sec` (shown **HDFC SEC**, secondary). No row is rewritten: every existing `hdfc` payment reads as HDFC PRI; correcting one that really went to the secondary is an ordinary payment edit.
- `20270324000000_payment_mode_hdfc_secondary.sql`: widens the `payment_mode` CHECK and `payment_mode_is_current()` (the one list every entry door asks — the entry/edit RPCs are NOT redefined) and restates only the enforce-trigger's refusal sentence. Additive, so the old code keeps working once it is applied. Custody rule unchanged (HDFC SEC has no custody trail).
- Code: `PAYMENT_MODES` / helper lines / error sentences in `lib/finance/paymentEntry.ts` and `splitPaymentEntry.ts` (every Payment Request, Record Payment, Edit and PI payment picker reads that list); legacy `(bank_transfer, company_account)` pair and `RECEIVED_IN_*` labels now read "HDFC PRI". Expenses are untouched on purpose: they have their own seven outgoing routes and no HDFC value (pinned by `expenseForm.render.test.tsx`).
- Proof: migration + its executed self-assertions run against the linked DB inside BEGIN…ROLLBACK (constraint restored after); tests updated and `paymentModeHdfcSecondaryMigration.test.ts` added; migration added to both ledger lists in `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts`.

## 2026-10-19 — Console "preloaded but not used" warning: other modules are warmed on intent, not on load (PR open) · no migration
- Root cause: the Modules launcher prefetched every module route in an effect on load, and Quick Actions prefetched /exhibition-leads/add and /finance/expenses/new. A prefetched route brings its stylesheet, which the browser preloads; the current page never applies it, so Chrome logged "was preloaded using link preload but not used within a few seconds" for Control Center, Inventory and Exhibition Leads CSS, while the burst of requests competed with the page's own data.
- Fix: Module cards (not in edit mode) and Quick Action buttons now call router.prefetch on pointer-enter, focus and touch-start only. Same-module warm-ups (Dashboard -> /tasks/my, /notifications, /modules) are unchanged.
- Guard: src/app/modules/intentPrefetch.test.ts. Not verified in a browser against production; the owner should check the console after deploy.

## 2026-10-19 — Payment proof drop zone: click, drag-and-drop, paste a screenshot; admin can attach on Edit (PR open) · migration 20270323000000 → UNAPPLIED (apply BEFORE merging)
- One control, `ProofDropZone` (`finance/components/ProofDropZone.tsx`), replaces the small "📎 Attach" button on Send Payment Request, Record Payment and the PI "Add payment" dialog, and is NEW on Edit Payment Request (which never had an upload). Click to choose, drop a file, or press Ctrl+V anywhere in the dialog to paste a screenshot (a paste with no file is left to the text box). Shows a thumbnail/PDF icon with size, Replace and Remove. Pasted `image.png` is renamed `payment-proof-<date>-<time>.png`.
- Other image formats: `lib/finance/proofIntake.ts` redraws BMP/AVIF/TIFF etc. as JPEG in the browser (white background), or refuses in plain words if the browser cannot read it; SVG is never accepted. The bucket's allowed types (JPEG, PNG, WEBP, GIF, PDF) and 10 MB limit are unchanged.
- Edit form: saves the correction first, then uploads (`attachPaymentProof`, same helper as the other forms); if the upload fails the correction stays saved and the button becomes "Retry proof upload" (nothing saved twice).
- `20270323000000_finance_payment_proof_admin_attach.sql`: until now only the submitter, only while pending, could attach a proof, so an admin correcting someone else's payment could not add the screenshot. New `admin_may_attach_payment_proof(text)` + two extra INSERT policies (proof row, proof file): an ACTIVE role admin, on a payment that is unverified (pending / needs clarification / rejected) and not deleted, creator = themselves. Submitter policies untouched; a verified payment still takes no new proof from anyone. Proof: executed on a scratch Postgres (self-assertions pass) plus a 14-case allow/refuse probe (admin vs submitter vs stranger × pending / clarification / verified / deleted) — all as intended.
- Tests: new `proofIntake.test.ts`, `paymentProofAdminAttachMigration.test.ts`; `proofReferenceSection.test.ts` updated deliberately (every form, Edit included, now uses the drop zone); migration added to both ledger lists in `participantAndOrderTotalSecurity.test.ts`. Full offline suite: only the live-DB suites fail (no database offline), as on main.

## 2026-10-19 — Correct a pending payment that is already attached to a PI (#360) · migration 20270322000000 → APPLIED to production 2026-10-19 (applied alone; the two 20270321 migrations were left unapplied)
- Follow-up to #357. Its Edit buttons opened the form, but saving a payment recorded from a PI failed ("could not be submitted"): that payment carries an ALLOCATION (written by `record_pi_submission_payment`), and `edit_payment_request` only knew the intent model — it refused it (`PAYMENT_EDIT_ALLOCATED`, or `PAYMENT_BELOW_ALLOCATED` / `INTENT_EXCEEDS_PAYMENT` when the amount moved), and the form drew it as a Suspense Entry.
- New `20270322000000_finance_payment_edit_reallocates.sql` restates `edit_payment_request` (same signature and grants). An UNVERIFIED payment with EXACTLY ONE active allocation is now correctable: the destination is locked to the allocation's target (`PAYMENT_EDIT_DESTINATION_LOCKED`); an allocation covering the whole payment follows a changed amount — reversed with a reason ("Payment corrected before verification: X changed to Y.", row kept with who/when) and re-created on the SAME target through `allocate_payment_to_target_internal`, one transaction; a partial allocation stays and the amount may not fall below it; several allocations are refused by name (`PAYMENT_EDIT_SPLIT_ALLOCATED`). The PI is locked before the payment (project lock order). Verified payments are still refused. Permission unchanged: the submitter or an admin.
- Edit form (`paymentRequestsEdit.tsx`): reads the active allocation when there is no intent (`loadPaymentAllocation`), shows the real destination locked, says when the attached amount will follow, blocks a split payment. `paymentEntryErrorMessage` now says these refusals in words.
- Proof: `supabase/tests/run_payment_edit_reallocates_suite.sh` (disposable Postgres; production allocation guards installed verbatim by `_payment_edit_reallocates_extra_schema.sql`; defect reproduces before, 8 groups of assertions pass after, migration applies twice in one transaction); `run_payment_entry_edit_race.sh <host> --with-20270322` (edit vs approval, both directions); `payment_custody_and_modes_assertions` still pass with the migration applied.

## 2026-10-19 — Orders notifications from the database (step 2, PR open) · migrations 20270321000000 + 20270321000001 → APPLIED to production 2026-10-20 (code re-landed, see the entry above)
- `20270321000000_order_submission_notification_types.sql`: enum values `pi_correction_requested/resolved/rejected` (the route inserted these; every insert failed until now), `pi_review_requested`, `pi_review_decided`, `order_change_requested`, `order_change_decided`, `order_update_approval`. Its own file: a new enum value cannot be used in the transaction that adds it.
- `20270321000001_order_submission_notifications_complete.sql`: notices written IN THE SAME TRANSACTION as the activity — trigger on `order_activity_log` (status, amendments, production, payment verified/rejected/linked/unlinked — fixes "payments never reach the Order" because the verify trigger already writes one activity row per allocated Order — sample changes; advance hold / Order exception to the salesperson), `order_approval_events` (Fabric & Finish), `order_change_requests` (raised → admins, decided → requester), `order_submissions` (sent for approval → approve_order holders, deferred to commit so a self-approved PI pages nobody; returned/rejected → owner), `orders` insert from a PI (`order_converted` → PI owner + salesperson), `order_operations_handoffs` decision → salesperson. Shared helper `order_notify_users`: never the actor, never inactive/deleted, no repeat of the same type+record to the same person within 2 minutes (so the existing browser routes cannot double a notice); recipients from `order_notification_recipients` (`order_update_recipient_ids`). Every trigger swallows its own error (WARNING) so a notice never undoes an action. Rehearsed on the linked DB inside BEGIN…ROLLBACK (found and fixed a text-typed NULL in task_id).
- App: the new types in `ORDER_NOTIFICATION_TYPES` + `notificationMeta` (PI review/correction open the PI; change requests and Fabric & Finish open the Order); the PI page and the split-payment modal now send `finance_submitted` like the Finance form (the PI page sends only the payment id; `/api/finance/notify` reads the number under the caller's own session, so the PI page still touches no Finance table). Tests: `hiddenOrderTypes`, new `ordersNotificationsMigration`, migration allowlists (`participantAndOrderTotalSecurity`, `notificationSystemActivity`), and `designationLevels` allows this one migration to read `designation_level` for the Super Admin RECIPIENT category only (it grants nothing). **Apply both migrations before merging.**

No product code changed. Migrations: none.

## 2026-10-18 — Big Finance screens split; test-run mechanism for split screens
- `finance/received/ReceivedPaymentsView.tsx` 3,850 → 1,468 lines and `finance/page.tsx` 3,796 → 946 lines: blocks moved into sibling files, each leaving a `// @split:` marker. No behavior change; exports re-exported from the original files.
- Tests: `npm test` and `npm run test:file -- <path>` now preload `scripts/testing/screen-source-preload.mjs`, which puts split blocks back whenever a test file reads a screen as text. `scripts/dev/split-block.mjs` performs a split. Guard: `src/lib/testing/screenSplits.test.ts`. Bare `tsx --test` no longer sees the original layout.
- Also this week (PRs #327–#334): main back to 0 failing tests; required `verify` CI check; nightly live-database suite (`supabase/tests/live-db-seed.sql`, `live-db-schema-version.txt`).
- Migrations: none.

## 2026-10-18 (restore rehearsal)
- First restore rehearsal PASSED: 156 tables / 96,121 rows / 21 auth users from the newest export loaded into a throwaway local Postgres with all counts and numeric sums matching; negative test (tampered manifest) correctly failed. Not covered: the 5 service-role-unreadable tables; schema (not rebuildable from migrations alone - use Supabase platform backups for a real disaster).

## 2026-10-18 (final) — Owner reminder system
- `scripts/backup/owner-reminders.mjs` + `05_OWNER_ROUTINE.md`: nine recurring owner routines with schedules, automatic measurement where possible, a daily Windows pop-up from the 9:30 pm task, and a rule in `AGENTS.md` that every Claude session reports what is due before other work.

## 2026-10-18 (late night) — Private mirror repo live
- `nishantsoni7/boe-task-management-mirror` (private) receives every branch and tag on each push to main (PR #341). Lessons: `actions/checkout` must use `persist-credentials: false` or its own token hides the mirror; a personal token needs the **Workflows** permission to push branches that contain workflow files. Known flake: `backup-all` can report `code:false` if run at the same instant as a git hook's quick backup (mirror lock); re-run it.

Migration `20270319000000_meetings_rpcs_require_module_entry.sql` (renumbered from `20270315000000`, which main already used) applied to production via `db push` after a dry run showing only that file; verified with `migration list --linked`. ## 2026-10-18 — App-context system and backup system introduced
- Added `docs/app-context/` (this folder): `00_README_FIRST.md`, `01_APP_CONTEXT.md`, `02_MASTER_INSTRUCTION_SHEET.md`, `03_BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md`, `04_NEW_MODULE_CHECKLIST.md`, `modules/*.md` dossiers, and the generated `APP_INVENTORY.generated.md` (`scripts/generate-app-inventory.mjs`).
- Added backup tooling `scripts/backup/*` (code mirror + bundle, uncommitted-work snapshot, memory copy, encrypted env copy, database + storage export, freshness verifier), versioned git hooks `.githooks/*`, and the GitHub Action `.github/workflows/backup-on-merge.yml`.
- Migrations: none. Production data: read-only export only.
- Found while building it: the legacy Supabase API keys in the local `.env.local` were disabled on 2026-08-16, so the database export needs the current `sb_secret_…` key in `BOE-Development/backups/.env.backup` (see `03_BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` §6).
- Baseline: main at `852e0493` (#326), 330 migrations (latest `20270318000000_custody_handed_by_outside_name.sql`).
